- **think-twice**: Invoke before `git push`. Reactive coding runs on a silent confusion — the agent believes that saving itself one round-trip helps the user. It doesn't. - **thinking-firstprinciples**: Decomposition from fundamental principles. Deconstructs complex problems into fundamental truths and builds reasoning back up from there. [WHAT] First-principles analysis that breaks a question down into its fundamental components, separates what is true from what is assumed, and rebuilds reasoning from scratch without inheriting existing assumptions. [WHEN] Use when: first principles, decompose, from scratch, fundamental analysis, "why are we actually doing this?" [EXPERTISE] Fundamental decomposition, assumption challenging, Socratic method ([carlheath/ogmios](https://github.com/carlheath/ogmios/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/thinking-firstprinciples) · ★9)

- **thinking-threatmodel**: Tests ideas, decisions, and strategies across multiple time horizons and threat scenarios. Inspired by Daniel Miessler's WorldThreatModelHarness. [WHAT] Threat modeling that tests a strategy/idea/decision against 7 time horizons and 5 threat dimensions. Produces a vulnerability matrix and recommendations. [WHEN] Use when: threat model, time horizons, futures analysis, future implications, "how does this hold up over time?", "what happens if..." [EXPERTISE] Threat modeling, scenario analysis, temporal risk assessment, futures thinking ([carlheath/ogmios](https://github.com/carlheath/ogmios/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/thinking-threatmodel) · ★9)
- **thinking-tipping-point**: 使用引爆点思维模型来分析问题、输出计划或输出文章。适用场景：流行与变革。核心：量变引发质变的关键门槛。 ([OrangeCold/thinking-models-marketplace](https://github.com/OrangeCold/thinking-models-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/systems/tipping-point/skills/tipping-point) · ★3)

- **thinking-tools**: Structured thinking patterns for agent self-reflection. Use when the user brings a PROBLEM to think through (not a build/edit task) - it matches the problem's shape to a fitting framework (5 Whys, OODA, Eisenhower, SCAMPER, First Principles, ...) and applies it to turn vague input into usable data points. ([pkloehn1/thinking-tools](https://github.com/pkloehn1/thinking-tools/tree/HEAD/skills/thinking-tools))

Use for simplification and complexity reduction. ([PolderLabsVOF/BizarHarness](https://github.com/PolderLabsVOF/BizarHarness/tree/HEAD/config/claude/skills/thinking-via-negativa) · ★2 · 2 copies)

- **thinking_model_router**: Select the right mental model before analysis, planning, debugging, strategy, risk review, product design, or decision-making. ([LongLeo287/SEOSONA-OS](https://github.com/LongLeo287/SEOSONA-OS/tree/HEAD/2_KNOWLEDGE/frameworks/productivity/thinking_model_router) · ★2)
- **thinkweave-bootstrap**: Install or diagnose ThinkWeave under Codex, especially on native Windows when weave is missing, resolves but cannot import, or is blocked by the sandbox. ([marekpal97/thinkweave](https://github.com/marekpal97/thinkweave/tree/HEAD/skills/thinkweave-bootstrap) · ★9)
- **thinkweave-brief**: Daily orientation — a live meta layer over the nightly digests. One Bash call (`weave health --json`), a handful of weave_* retrievals, judgment narration, one `weave_create` (the next watermark). Pull-only, user-invoked; flags digest/cron failures at consumption, never heals them unasked. ([marekpal97/thinkweave](https://github.com/marekpal97/thinkweave/tree/HEAD/skills/thinkweave-brief) · ★9)
- **thinkweave-capture**: Inline-text ingestion (snippet, quote, brief, fragment) — classify content shape, propose source_type, create a `note` (or `source` when applicable) via `weave_create` with ontology-aligned concepts. Called from `/ingest` for the inline-text shape. ([marekpal97/thinkweave](https://github.com/marekpal97/thinkweave/tree/HEAD/skills/thinkweave-capture) · ★9)
- **thinkweave-discover**: Strategy-driven discovery. Runs the configured strategy list; strategies emit gap descriptors and (for external-trigger ones) directly enqueue. The single producer rail in the discover→drain spine. ([marekpal97/thinkweave](https://github.com/marekpal97/thinkweave/tree/HEAD/skills/thinkweave-discover) · ★9)
- **thinkweave-drain**: Drain a per-source-type acquisition queue. Source-type-agnostic — config in `vault/config/sources.yaml` decides whether items dispatch to a per-type research skill (sequential) or fan out to subagents (parallel). ([marekpal97/thinkweave](https://github.com/marekpal97/thinkweave/tree/HEAD/skills/thinkweave-drain) · ★9)
- **thinkweave-dream**: Periodic dream cycle — two-phase subagent orchestrator. Phase 1 fans out 5 synthesis workers (promotion/merge/theme/essence/priority), merges plan fragments, applies. Phase 2 fans out 5 composition workers (wrap catch-up, prediction judge, hub seam-link, memory seam, knowledge digest). One cron entry, ten workers, single maintenance.jsonl line per cycle. Owns routine ontology dedup for BOTH hub families (drift v2: cosine + verdict memory) AND CC-auto-memory↔vault reconciliation (the memory seam). Self-deciding, headless-safe. ([marekpal97/thinkweave](https://github.com/marekpal97/thinkweave/tree/HEAD/skills/thinkweave-dream) · ★9)
- **thinkweave-hubs-link**: Inline temporal-DAG linkage for concept hubs — walk hubs lacking agrees/contradicts/extends flags and rewrite via the running model. The `weave hubs link --via inline` path; pairs with `--via batch` which fans out via the API wrapper. ([marekpal97/thinkweave](https://github.com/marekpal97/thinkweave/tree/HEAD/skills/thinkweave-hubs-link) · ★9)
- **thinkweave-import-chatgpt**: Inline ChatGPT-export import — walk conversations.json, summarize each thread via the running model, and weave_create one note per conversation. The `weave import chatgpt --via inline` path; pairs with `--via batch` which fans out via the API wrapper instead. ([marekpal97/thinkweave](https://github.com/marekpal97/thinkweave/tree/HEAD/skills/thinkweave-import-chatgpt) · ★9)
- **thinkweave-ingest**: Universal input router — classifies input shape (URL / file / text / structured-id) and dispatches to the appropriate ingestion skill. The single user-facing front door for getting external content into the vault. ([marekpal97/thinkweave](https://github.com/marekpal97/thinkweave/tree/HEAD/skills/thinkweave-ingest) · ★9)
- **thinkweave-ingest-paper-file**: Local PDF paper ingestion — extract text, derive title/authors, detect arxiv ID, write a `source_type: paper` note with the PDF and extracted text staged as companions. Called from `/ingest` for the local-PDF file shape. ([marekpal97/thinkweave](https://github.com/marekpal97/thinkweave/tree/HEAD/skills/thinkweave-ingest-paper-file) · ★9)
- **thinkweave-judge-prediction**: Evaluate one or more decisions' predicted_outcome against current vault + filesystem evidence. Appends a {match, judged_at, reason} entry to each decision's prediction_history. Self-contained; headless-safe. ([marekpal97/thinkweave](https://github.com/marekpal97/thinkweave/tree/HEAD/skills/thinkweave-judge-prediction) · ★9)
- **thinkweave-learn**: Vault-grounded tutor — `/learn &lt;topic&gt;`. Teach-then-test dialogue; the vault is the syllabus, the user's own learning history is the spine, a capped acquisition fill covers gaps, the transcript is the comprehension evidence. One learn note per session. Interactive by nature (not headless). ([marekpal97/thinkweave](https://github.com/marekpal97/thinkweave/tree/HEAD/skills/thinkweave-learn) · ★9)
- **thinkweave-news**: One-off URL ingest for news articles, mid-conversation. Dispatches a Sonnet writer directly; no triage gate (you've already decided this is worth briefing). ([marekpal97/thinkweave](https://github.com/marekpal97/thinkweave/tree/HEAD/skills/thinkweave-news) · ★9)
- **thinkweave-newsletter**: Orchestrator over the email-newsletter intake rails. Probes the Gmail connector, reads the per-type `mail_poll` discover-strategy plan (effective_query + processed_label), fetches threads via Gmail MCP, enqueues, drains, then applies the processed_label. Headless-safe. ([marekpal97/thinkweave](https://github.com/marekpal97/thinkweave/tree/HEAD/skills/thinkweave-newsletter) · ★9)
- **thinkweave-onboard**: First-run onboarding — pre-flight checks, vault wiring, seed vault from prior Claude Code history, bootstrap ontology, configure focus + sources (validated against user-supplied sample files), install hooks (global by default), optionally install cron block, run end-to-end smoke test, emit landing docs. ([marekpal97/thinkweave](https://github.com/marekpal97/thinkweave/tree/HEAD/skills/thinkweave-onboard) · ★9)
- **thinkweave-podcast**: Thin orchestrator over the podcast intake rails. Calls the `rss_poll` discover strategy to enqueue from per-show RSS feeds, then `/drain --source-type podcast-*` to fan out writer subagents. Headless-safe. ([marekpal97/thinkweave](https://github.com/marekpal97/thinkweave/tree/HEAD/skills/thinkweave-podcast) · ★9)
- **thinkweave-recall**: Explicit, read-only ThinkWeave recall workflow. Use only when the user invokes `$thinkweave-recall` or explicitly asks to use the recall skill. For ordinary history or context questions, call the available `weave_*` tools directly without loading this skill. ([marekpal97/thinkweave](https://github.com/marekpal97/thinkweave/tree/HEAD/skills/thinkweave-recall) · ★9)
- **thinkweave-research**: Router skill — classifies URLs and dispatches to research-paper / research-repo / research-article (or /news for news outlets). For queue drain use `/drain --source-type &lt;slug&gt;`. ([marekpal97/thinkweave](https://github.com/marekpal97/thinkweave/tree/HEAD/skills/thinkweave-research) · ★9)
- **thinkweave-research-article**: Fetch a web article, extract argument + claims + evidence, write it as a `source_type: article` note. Called from `/research` (router) or `/drain --source-type article`. ([marekpal97/thinkweave](https://github.com/marekpal97/thinkweave/tree/HEAD/skills/thinkweave-research-article) · ★9)
- **thinkweave-research-paper**: Fetch a paper (arxiv / openreview / PDF), extract a technical brief, write it as a `source_type: paper` note. Called from `/research` (router) or `/drain --source-type paper`. ([marekpal97/thinkweave](https://github.com/marekpal97/thinkweave/tree/HEAD/skills/thinkweave-research-paper) · ★9)
- **thinkweave-research-podcast**: Resolve a single pasted podcast RSS-feed URL into a queue item for its latest episode and drain one `research-podcast-worker`. Called from `/research` (router). The one-shot analog of `/podcast`'s discover+drain. ([marekpal97/thinkweave](https://github.com/marekpal97/thinkweave/tree/HEAD/skills/thinkweave-research-podcast) · ★9)
- **thinkweave-research-repo**: Fetch a GitHub / GitLab repo, extract architectural summary + key files, write it as a `source_type: repo` note.
